"""LibreTorrent 3.5.2 schema-2 trigger graph.

This file is being rebuilt from the uploaded source.  The generated draft is
preserved separately and is not imported here.
"""

TITLE = 'LibreTorrent 3.5.2 trigger graph — source re-audit'
STRICT = False
USE_AUTO = True
PREFER_SETUP_OVER_ENV = True

# These bytecode call pairs have no route-bearing caller.  Suppress them from
# the inventory-derived auto edges after confirming their source call sites.
SUPPRESS = {
    ('NanoHTTPD.decodeParameters(Map)', 'NanoHTTPD.decodeParameters(String)'),
    ('NanoHTTPD.mimeTypes', 'NanoHTTPD.loadMimeTypes'),
    ('NanoHTTPD.makeSSLSocketFactory(String,char[])', 'NanoHTTPD.makeSSLSocketFactory(KeyStore,KeyManagerFactory)'),
    ('NanoHTTPD.makeSSLSocketFactory(KeyStore,KeyManagerFactory)', 'NanoHTTPD.makeSSLSocketFactory(KeyStore,KeyManager[])'),
    ('NanoHTTPD.getMimeTypeForFile', 'NanoHTTPD.mimeTypes'),
    ('NanoHTTPD.isAlive', 'NanoHTTPD.wasStarted'),
    ('NanoHTTPD$Cookie.<init>(String,String)', 'NanoHTTPD$Cookie.<init>(String,String,int)'),
    ('NanoHTTPD.makeSecure', 'NanoHTTPD$SecureServerSocketFactory.<init>'),
    ('TorrentEngine.addTorrentSync(AddTorrentParams,boolean)', 'TorrentSessionImpl.addTorrent'),
    ('TorrentSessionImpl.addTorrent', 'TorrentSessionImpl.download(String,AddTorrentParams,byte[])'),
}

# Remove inventory-generated component edges that the printed app source
# disproves: receivers are internal and PendingIntent cancellation is not a
# receiver invocation.
_GENERATED_EDGE_DROPS = {
    ('S_ENTRY', 'S_SYS_NotificationReceiver', 'ENTRY'),
    ('S_ENTRY', 'S_SYS_SchedulerReceiver', 'ENTRY'),
    ('Scheduler.cancelStartAppAlarm', 'S_SYS_SchedulerReceiver', 'INTENT'),
    ('Scheduler.cancelStopAppAlarm', 'S_SYS_SchedulerReceiver', 'INTENT'),
}
_append_edge = e

def _filter_generated_edge(src, dst, kind, trigger, *args, **kwargs):
    if (src, dst, kind) in _GENERATED_EDGE_DROPS:
        return
    return _append_edge(src, dst, kind, trigger, *args, **kwargs)

e = _filter_generated_edge

# Temporary only while S3/S4 inventory is being classified.  It is removed
# before STRICT is enabled; no AUTO_DEAD result is accepted as a final label.
AUTO_DEAD = 'S3/S4 inventory only: source classification still required.'

def _source_audit_edit(edge):
    """Do not represent generated framework guesses as remembered facts."""
    evidence = edge.get('evidence') or ''
    if 'FW-MEMORY' in evidence:
        edge['evidence'] = evidence.replace('FW-MEMORY', 'INFERRED')

    # Exact source predicates/texts replace generator prose gates. The kind
    # maps below classify these exact strings after this edit runs.
    if edge.get('src') == 'S_ENTRY' and edge.get('dst') == 'S_SYS_BootReceiver':
        edge['env'] = 'isBootCompleted(action)'
    elif edge.get('src') == 'S_ENTRY' and edge.get('dst') in {
        'MainActivity.onCreate', 'MainActivity.<init>',
        'SendTextToClipboard.<init>', 'SendTextToClipboard.onCreate',
        'AddFeedActivity.<init>', 'AddFeedActivity.onCreate',
        'AddTorrentActivity.<init>', 'AddTorrentActivity.onCreate',
        'SettingsActivity.<init>', 'SettingsActivity.onCreate'
    }:
        edge['env'] = 'android:exported="true"'
    elif edge.get('src') == 'TorrentNotifier.makeTorrentFinishedNotify' and edge.get('dst') == 'DetailTorrentActivity.<init>':
        edge['env'] = 'openPendingIntent'
    elif edge.get('src') == 'TorrentService.initPendingIntents' and edge.get('dst') == 'S_SYS_NotificationReceiver':
        edge['env'] = 'PendingIntent.getBroadcast('
    elif edge.get('src') == 'Scheduler.setStartStopAppAlarm' and edge.get('dst') == 'S_SYS_SchedulerReceiver':
        edge['env'] = 'calendar.getTimeInMillis()'

    if edge.get('needs_state') == 'The list / adapter shows at least one row (some user data exists: add an item first)':
        edge['needs_state'] = 'getAdapter().getItemCount()'


EDIT = _source_audit_edit

    def e(src, dst, kind, trigger, gui=(), guard=None, reads=(), writes=(), note=None,
          needs_state=None, evidence=None, env=None, auto=False, cls=None, via=None, env_kinds=(), state_kinds=()):
        x = {'src': src, 'dst': dst, 'kind': kind, 'trigger': trigger, 'gui': list(gui),
             'guard': guard, 'reads': list(reads), 'writes': list(writes), 'note': note,
             'needs_state': needs_state, 'evidence': evidence, 'env': env,
             'edge_class': cls, 'via': via, 'env_kinds': list(env_kinds), 'state_kinds': list(state_kinds)}
        if auto:
            x['auto'] = True
        edges.append(x)

    auto_path = os.path.join(gdir, 'auto_calls.json')
    auto_data = json.load(open(auto_path)) if os.path.isfile(auto_path) else None

    def impl(cls, name, desc=None):
        """Node that runs when method `name` is invoked on an instance of class `cls` (short name)."""
        tbl = (auto_data or {}).get('impl', {}).get(cls, {}).get(name)
        if not tbl:
            raise KeyError('impl: %s has no method %s' % (cls, name))
        if desc:
            return tbl[desc]
        if len(set(tbl.values())) != 1:
            raise KeyError('impl: %s.%s is overloaded, pass desc: %s' % (cls, name, sorted(tbl)))
        return next(iter(tbl.values()))

package org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent;

import android.annotation.SuppressLint;
import android.database.CursorWindow;
import android.util.Log;

import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatDelegate;
import androidx.multidex.MultiDexApplication;

import org.acra.ACRA;
import org.acra.config.CoreConfigurationBuilder;
import org.acra.config.DialogConfigurationBuilder;
import org.acra.config.MailSenderConfigurationBuilder;
import org.acra.data.StringFormat;
import org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.ui.TorrentNotifier;
import org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.ui.errorreport.ErrorReportActivity;

public class MainApplication extends MultiDexApplication {
    public static final String TAG = MainApplication.class.getSimpleName();

    static {
        /* Vector Drawable support in ImageView for API < 21 */
        AppCompatDelegate.setCompatVectorFromResourcesEnabled(true);
    }

    @Override
    public void onCreate() {
        super.onCreate();

        CoreConfigurationBuilder builder = new CoreConfigurationBuilder();
        builder
                .withBuildConfigClass(BuildConfig.class)
                .withReportFormat(StringFormat.JSON);
        builder.withPluginConfigurations(new MailSenderConfigurationBuilder()
                .withMailTo("proninyaroslav@mail.ru")
                .build());
        builder.withPluginConfigurations(new DialogConfigurationBuilder()
                .withEnabled(true)
                .withReportDialogClass(ErrorReportActivity.class)
                .build());
        // Set stub handler
        if (Thread.getDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler() == null) {
            Thread.setDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler((t, e) ->
                    Log.e(TAG, "Uncaught exception in " + t + ": " + Log.getStackTraceString(e))
            );
        }
        ACRA.init(this, builder);

        increaseCursorWindowSize();

        TorrentNotifier.getInstance(this).makeNotifyChans();
    }

    @SuppressLint("DiscouragedPrivateApi")
    private void increaseCursorWindowSize() {
        try {
            var field = CursorWindow.class.getDeclaredField("sCursorWindowSize");
            field.setAccessible(true);
            field.set(null, 100 * 1024 * 1024); // 100MB
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Unable to increase CursorWindow size", e);
        }
    }

package org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core;

import android.content.Context;
import android.text.TextUtils;

import androidx.annotation.NonNull;

import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Enclosure;
import com.ernieyu.feedparser.EzRssTorrentItem;
import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Feed;
import com.ernieyu.feedparser.FeedParserFactory;
import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Item;
import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.Content;
import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.Hash;
import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.MediaRss;

import org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core.model.data.entity.FeedChannel;
import org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core.model.data.entity.FeedItem;
import org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core.utils.Utils;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.List;

/*
 * Parser RSS/Atom feeds.
 *
 * Tags are supported for items:
 *  - <link>
 *  - <enclosure>
 *  - <media:content>
 *  - <media:hash>
 *  - <guid> (sometimes the torrent link is encoded as the GUID in RSS feeds)
 *  - ezRSS <torrent: ... > namespace
 */

public class FeedParser
{
    private FeedChannel feedChannel;
    private Feed feed;

    public FeedParser(@NonNull Context context, @NonNull FeedChannel feedChannel) throws Exception
    {
        this.feedChannel = feedChannel;
        ByteArrayInputStream bsStream = null;
        try {
            byte[] response = Utils.fetchHttpUrl(context, feedChannel.url);
            if (response == null)
                return;
            bsStream = new ByteArrayInputStream(response);
            feed = FeedParserFactory.newParser().parse(bsStream);

        } finally {
            if (bsStream != null) {
                try {
                    bsStream.close();
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    /* Ignore */
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public String getTitle()
    {
        return feed.getTitle();
    }

    public List<FeedItem> getItems()
    {
        List<FeedItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
        if (feed == null)
            return items;

        for (Item item : feed.getItemList()) {
            List<String> links = item.getLinks();
            String articleUrl = getFirstNotNullLink(links);
            String downloadUrl = watchDownloadableLink(links);

            /* Find url with torrent/magnet */
            if (downloadUrl == null)
                downloadUrl = findDownloadUrl(item);

            Date pubDate = item.getPubDate();
            long pubDateTime = 0;
            if (pubDate != null)
                pubDateTime = pubDate.getTime();

            FeedItem feedItem = new FeedItem(feedChannel.id, downloadUrl,
                    articleUrl, item.getTitle(), pubDateTime);
            feedItem.fetchDate = System.currentTimeMillis();
            items.add(feedItem);
        }

        return items;
    }

    private String getFirstNotNullLink(List<String> links)
    {
        for (String link : links) {
            if (!TextUtils.isEmpty(link))
                return link;
        }

        return null;
    }

    private String watchDownloadableLink(List<String> links)
    {
        for (String link : links) {
            if (link == null)
                continue;

            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(link))
                return link;
        }

        return null;
    }

$ sed -n '141,267p' "final_java_only/LibreTorrent/app/LibreTorrent-#3.5.2/app/src/main/java/org/proninyaroslav/libretorrent/core/FeedParser.java"
    private String findDownloadUrl(Item item)
    {
        /*
         * Watch tags in descending order of importance
         * (hashes in the last place)
         */
        String enclosureUrl = watchEnclosure(item);
        if (enclosureUrl != null)
            return enclosureUrl;

        String infoHash = watchTorrentInfoHash(item);
        if (infoHash != null)
            return infoHash;

        String mediaContentUrl = watchMediaContent(item);
        if (mediaContentUrl != null)
            return mediaContentUrl;

        String guid = watchGuid(item);
        return guid;
    }

    /*
     * Parse element like this:
     * <enclosure type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
     */

    private String watchEnclosure(Item item)
    {
        for (Enclosure enclosure : item.getEnclosures()) {
            if (enclosure == null)
                continue;

            String url = enclosure.getUrl();
            String type = enclosure.getType();
            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
                return url;
        }

        return null;
    }

    /*
     * Parse element like this:
     * <media:content type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
     */

    private String watchMediaContent(Item item)
    {
        MediaRss media = item.getMediaRss();
        if (media == null)
            return null;

        List<Content> contentList = media.getContent();
        for (Content content : contentList) {
            if (content == null)
                continue;

            String url = content.getUrl();
            if (url == null)
                return watchMediaHash(media);
            String type = content.getType();
            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
                return url;
        }

        return watchMediaHash(media);
    }

    /*
     * Parse element like this:
     * <media:hash algo="sha1">8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</media:hash>
     */

    private String watchMediaHash(MediaRss media)
    {
        Hash hash = media.getHash();
        if (hash == null)
            return null;

        String hashStr = hash.getValue();
        if (hashStr == null)
            return null;
        String algo = hash.getAlgorithm();
        if (Utils.isHash(hashStr) && algo != null && algo.equalsIgnoreCase("sha1"))
            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(hashStr);

        return null;
    }

    /*
     * Parse element like this:
     * <torrent:infoHash>8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</torrent:infoHash>
     */

    private String watchTorrentInfoHash(Item item)
    {
        EzRssTorrentItem torrentItem = item.getEzRssTorrentItem();
        if (torrentItem == null)
            return null;

        String infoHash = torrentItem.getInfoHash();
        if (infoHash != null && Utils.isHash(infoHash))
            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(infoHash);

        return null;
    }

    /*
     * Parse element like this:
     * <guid>http://foo.com/bar.torrent</guid>
     */

    private String watchGuid(Item item)
    {
        String url = item.getGuid();
        if (url != null && isMagnetOrTorrent(url))
            return url;

        return null;
    }

    private boolean isMagnetOrTorrent(String url)
    {
        return url.endsWith(".torrent") || url.startsWith(Utils.MAGNET_PREFIX);
    }
}

57:public class FeedParser
58:{
59:    private FeedChannel feedChannel;
60:    private Feed feed;
61:
62:    public FeedParser(@NonNull Context context, @NonNull FeedChannel feedChannel) throws Exception
63:    {
64:        this.feedChannel = feedChannel;
65:        ByteArrayInputStream bsStream = null;
66:        try {
67:            byte[] response = Utils.fetchHttpUrl(context, feedChannel.url);
68:            if (response == null)
69:                return;
70:            bsStream = new ByteArrayInputStream(response);
71:            feed = FeedParserFactory.newParser().parse(bsStream);
72:
73:        } finally {
74:            if (bsStream != null) {
75:                try {
76:                    bsStream.close();
77:                } catch (Exception e) {
78:                    /* Ignore */
79:                }
80:            }
81:        }
82:    }
83:
84:    public String getTitle()
85:    {
86:        return feed.getTitle();
87:    }
88:
89:    public List<FeedItem> getItems()
90:    {
91:        List<FeedItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
92:        if (feed == null)
93:            return items;
94:
95:        for (Item item : feed.getItemList()) {
96:            List<String> links = item.getLinks();
97:            String articleUrl = getFirstNotNullLink(links);
98:            String downloadUrl = watchDownloadableLink(links);
99:
100:            /* Find url with torrent/magnet */
101:            if (downloadUrl == null)
102:                downloadUrl = findDownloadUrl(item);
103:
104:            Date pubDate = item.getPubDate();
105:            long pubDateTime = 0;
106:            if (pubDate != null)
107:                pubDateTime = pubDate.getTime();
108:
109:            FeedItem feedItem = new FeedItem(feedChannel.id, downloadUrl,
110:                    articleUrl, item.getTitle(), pubDateTime);
111:            feedItem.fetchDate = System.currentTimeMillis();
112:            items.add(feedItem);
113:        }
114:
115:        return items;
116:    }
117:
118:    private String getFirstNotNullLink(List<String> links)
119:    {
120:        for (String link : links) {
121:            if (!TextUtils.isEmpty(link))
122:                return link;
123:        }
124:
125:        return null;
126:    }
127:
128:    private String watchDownloadableLink(List<String> links)
129:    {
130:        for (String link : links) {
131:            if (link == null)
132:                continue;
133:
134:            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(link))
135:                return link;
136:        }
137:
138:        return null;
139:    }

$ grep -n '' "final_java_only/LibreTorrent/app/LibreTorrent-#3.5.2/app/src/main/java/org/proninyaroslav/libretorrent/core/FeedParser.java" | sed -n '141,267p'
141:    private String findDownloadUrl(Item item)
142:    {
143:        /*
144:         * Watch tags in descending order of importance
145:         * (hashes in the last place)
146:         */
147:        String enclosureUrl = watchEnclosure(item);
148:        if (enclosureUrl != null)
149:            return enclosureUrl;
150:
151:        String infoHash = watchTorrentInfoHash(item);
152:        if (infoHash != null)
153:            return infoHash;
154:
155:        String mediaContentUrl = watchMediaContent(item);
156:        if (mediaContentUrl != null)
157:            return mediaContentUrl;
158:
159:        String guid = watchGuid(item);
160:        return guid;
161:    }
162:
163:    /*
164:     * Parse element like this:
165:     * <enclosure type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
166:     */
167:
168:    private String watchEnclosure(Item item)
169:    {
170:        for (Enclosure enclosure : item.getEnclosures()) {
171:            if (enclosure == null)
172:                continue;
173:
174:            String url = enclosure.getUrl();
175:            String type = enclosure.getType();
176:            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
177:                return url;
178:        }
179:
180:        return null;
181:    }
182:
183:    /*
184:     * Parse element like this:
185:     * <media:content type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
186:     */
187:
188:    private String watchMediaContent(Item item)
189:    {
190:        MediaRss media = item.getMediaRss();
191:        if (media == null)
192:            return null;
193:
194:        List<Content> contentList = media.getContent();
195:        for (Content content : contentList) {
196:            if (content == null)
197:                continue;
198:
199:            String url = content.getUrl();
200:            if (url == null)
201:                return watchMediaHash(media);
202:            String type = content.getType();
203:            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
204:                return url;
205:        }
206:
207:        return watchMediaHash(media);
208:    }
209:
210:    /*
211:     * Parse element like this:
212:     * <media:hash algo="sha1">8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</media:hash>
213:     */
214:
215:    private String watchMediaHash(MediaRss media)
216:    {
217:        Hash hash = media.getHash();
218:        if (hash == null)
219:            return null;
220:
221:        String hashStr = hash.getValue();
222:        if (hashStr == null)
223:            return null;
224:        String algo = hash.getAlgorithm();
225:        if (Utils.isHash(hashStr) && algo != null && algo.equalsIgnoreCase("sha1"))
226:            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(hashStr);
227:
228:        return null;
229:    }
230:
231:    /*
232:     * Parse element like this:
233:     * <torrent:infoHash>8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</torrent:infoHash>
234:     */
235:
236:    private String watchTorrentInfoHash(Item item)
237:    {
238:        EzRssTorrentItem torrentItem = item.getEzRssTorrentItem();
239:        if (torrentItem == null)
240:            return null;
241:
242:        String infoHash = torrentItem.getInfoHash();
243:        if (infoHash != null && Utils.isHash(infoHash))
244:            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(infoHash);
245:
246:        return null;
247:    }
248:
249:    /*
250:     * Parse element like this:
251:     * <guid>http://foo.com/bar.torrent</guid>
252:     */
253:
254:    private String watchGuid(Item item)
255:    {
256:        String url = item.getGuid();
257:        if (url != null && isMagnetOrTorrent(url))
258:            return url;
259:
260:        return null;
261:    }
262:
263:    private boolean isMagnetOrTorrent(String url)
264:    {
265:        return url.endsWith(".torrent") || url.startsWith(Utils.MAGNET_PREFIX);
266:    }
267:}

126:    {

135:        }

152:            return Result.failure();

154:        FeedParser parser;
155:        try {
156:            parser = new FeedParser(getApplicationContext(), channel);
157:
158:        } catch (Exception e) {
159:            channel.fetchError = e.getMessage();
160:            repo.updateFeed(channel);
161:
162:            return Result.failure();
163:        }
164:

166:

168:
169:        if (pref.feedRemoveDuplicates())
170:            filterItemDuplicates(items);
171:
172:        repo.addItems(items);
173:
174:        channel.fetchError = null;
175:        if (TextUtils.isEmpty(channel.name)) {
176:            channel.name = parser.getTitle();
177:            if (TextUtils.isEmpty(channel.name))
178:                channel.name = channel.url;
179:        }
180:        channel.lastUpdate = System.currentTimeMillis();

21:package org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core;
22:
23:import org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core.utils.Utils;
24:
25:import java.io.IOException;
26:import java.net.HttpURLConnection;
27:import java.net.MalformedURLException;
28:import java.net.URL;
29:import java.security.GeneralSecurityException;
30:
31:import javax.net.ssl.HttpsURLConnection;
32:import javax.net.ssl.SSLContext;
33:
34:import static android.text.format.DateUtils.SECOND_IN_MILLIS;
35:import static java.net.HttpURLConnection.HTTP_MOVED_PERM;
36:import static java.net.HttpURLConnection.HTTP_MOVED_TEMP;
37:import static java.net.HttpURLConnection.HTTP_SEE_OTHER;
38:
39:/*
40: * The wrapper for HttpUrlConnection.
41: */
42:
43:public class HttpConnection implements Runnable
44:{
45:    /* Can't be more than 7 */
46:    private static final int MAX_REDIRECTS = 5;
47:    private static final int DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = (int)(20 * SECOND_IN_MILLIS);
48:
49:    private URL url;
50:    private SSLContext sslContext;
51:    private Listener listener;
52:
53:    public interface Listener
54:    {
55:        void onConnectionCreated(HttpURLConnection conn);
56:
57:        void onResponseHandle(HttpURLConnection conn, int code, String message);
58:
59:        void onMovedPermanently(String newUrl);
60:
61:        void onIOException(IOException e);
62:
63:        void onTooManyRedirects();
64:    }
65:
66:    public HttpConnection(String url) throws MalformedURLException, GeneralSecurityException
67:    {
68:        this.url = new URL(url);
69:        this.sslContext = Utils.getSSLContext();
70:    }
71:
72:    public void setListener(Listener listener)
73:    {
74:        this.listener = listener;
75:    }
76:
77:    @Override
78:    public void run()
79:    {
80:        int redirectionCount = 0;
81:        while (redirectionCount++ < MAX_REDIRECTS) {
82:            HttpURLConnection conn = null;
83:            try {
84:                conn = (HttpURLConnection)url.openConnection();
85:                conn.setInstanceFollowRedirects(false);
86:                conn.setConnectTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT);
87:                conn.setReadTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT);
88:
89:                if (conn instanceof HttpsURLConnection)
90:                    ((HttpsURLConnection)conn).setSSLSocketFactory(sslContext.getSocketFactory());
91:
92:                if (listener != null)
93:                    listener.onConnectionCreated(conn);
94:
95:                int responseCode = conn.getResponseCode();
96:                switch (responseCode) {
97:                    case HTTP_MOVED_PERM:
98:                    case HTTP_MOVED_TEMP:
99:                    case HTTP_SEE_OTHER:
100:                        String location = conn.getHeaderField("Location");
101:                        url = new URL(url, location);
102:                        if (responseCode == HTTP_MOVED_PERM && listener != null)
103:                            listener.onMovedPermanently(url.toString());
104:                        continue;
105:                    default:
106:                        if (listener != null)
107:                            listener.onResponseHandle(conn, responseCode, conn.getResponseMessage());
108:                        return;
109:                }
110:
111:            } catch (IOException e) {
112:                if (listener != null)
113:                    listener.onIOException(e);
114:                return;
115:
116:            } finally {
117:                if (conn != null)
118:                    conn.disconnect();
119:            }
120:        }
121:
122:        if (listener != null)
123:            listener.onTooManyRedirects();
124:    }
125:}

462:    public static byte[] fetchHttpUrl(@NonNull Context context,
463:                                      @NonNull String url) throws FetchLinkException {
464:        byte[][] response = new byte[1][];
465:
466:        if (!Utils.checkConnectivity(context))
467:            throw new FetchLinkException("No network connection");
468:
469:        final ArrayList<Throwable> errorArray = new ArrayList<>(1);
470:        HttpConnection connection;
471:        try {
472:            connection = new HttpConnection(url);
473:        } catch (Exception e) {
474:            throw new FetchLinkException(e);
475:        }
476:
477:        connection.setListener(new HttpConnection.Listener() {
478:            @Override
479:            public void onConnectionCreated(HttpURLConnection conn) {
480:                /* Nothing */
481:            }
482:
483:            @Override
484:            public void onResponseHandle(HttpURLConnection conn, int code, String message) {
485:                if (code == HttpURLConnection.HTTP_OK) {
486:                    try(InputStream is = conn.getInputStream()) {
487:                        response[0] = IOUtils.toByteArray(is);
488:
489:                    } catch (IOException e) {
490:                        errorArray.add(e);
491:                    }
492:                } else {
493:                    errorArray.add(new FetchLinkException("Failed to fetch link, response code: " + code));
494:                }
495:            }
496:
497:            @Override
498:            public void onMovedPermanently(String newUrl) {
499:                /* Nothing */
500:            }
501:
502:            @Override
503:            public void onIOException(IOException e) {
504:                errorArray.add(e);
505:            }
506:
507:            @Override
508:            public void onTooManyRedirects() {
509:                errorArray.add(new FetchLinkException("Too many redirects"));
510:            }
511:        });
512:        connection.run();
513:
514:        if (!errorArray.isEmpty()) {
515:            StringBuilder s = new StringBuilder();
516:            for (Throwable e : errorArray) {
517:                String msg = e.getMessage();
518:                if (msg != null)
519:                    s.append(msg.concat("\n"));
520:            }

20:package org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core;
21:
22:import android.text.InputFilter;
23:import android.text.Spanned;
24:
25:import androidx.annotation.Nullable;
26:
27:/*
28: * Filtering numbers, which are outside the specified range.
29: */
30:
31:public class InputFilterRange implements InputFilter
32:{
33:    public static final InputFilterRange PORT_FILTER = new InputFilterRange.Builder()
34:            .setMin(0)
35:            .setMax(65535)
36:            .build();
37:
38:    public static final InputFilterRange UNSIGNED_INT = new InputFilterRange.Builder()
39:            .setMin(0)
40:            .setMax(Integer.MAX_VALUE)
41:            .build();
42:
43:    private Integer min;
44:    private Integer max;
45:
46:    private InputFilterRange(Integer min, Integer max)
47:    {
48:        this.min = min;
49:        this.max = max;
50:    }
51:
52:    @Nullable
53:    public Integer getMin()
54:    {
55:        return min;
56:    }
57:
58:    @Nullable
59:    public Integer getMax()
60:    {
61:        return max;
62:    }
63:
64:    @Override
65:    public CharSequence filter(CharSequence source, int start, int end,
66:                               Spanned dest, int dstart, int dend)
67:    {
68:        if (source.length() == 1 && source.charAt(0) == '-')
69:            return null;
70:
71:        try {
72:            int input = Integer.parseInt(dest.toString() + source.toString());
73:            if (inRange(input))
74:                return null;
75:
76:        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
77:            /* Ignore */
78:        }
79:
80:        return "";
81:    }
82:
83:    private boolean inRange(int num)
84:    {
85:        return (min == null || num >= min) && (max == null || num <= max);
86:    }
87:
88:    public static class Builder
89:    {
90:        private Integer min;
91:        private Integer max;
92:
93:        public Builder setMin(int min)
94:        {
95:            this.min = min;
96:
97:            return this;
98:        }
99:
100:        public Builder setMax(int max)
101:        {
102:            this.max = max;
103:
104:            return this;
105:        }
106:
107:        public InputFilterRange build()
108:        {
109:            if (min != null && max != null && min > max)
110:                throw new IllegalArgumentException("max < min");
111:
112:            return new InputFilterRange(min, max);
113:        }
114:    }
115:}

20:package org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core;
21:

24:
25:import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
26:
27:import org.libtorrent4j.Pair;
28:import org.libtorrent4j.swig.create_flags_t;

30:import org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core.system.SystemFacadeHelper;
31:
32:import java.io.File;
33:import java.util.ArrayList;
34:import java.util.List;
35:
36:import io.reactivex.Observable;
37:import io.reactivex.Single;
38:import io.reactivex.functions.Predicate;
39:import io.reactivex.subjects.BehaviorSubject;
40:
41:public class TorrentBuilder
42:{
43:    private Context context;
44:    private org.libtorrent4j.TorrentBuilder builder;
45:    private Predicate<String> fileNameFilter;
46:    private BehaviorSubject<Progress> progress = BehaviorSubject.create();
47:
48:    public static class Tracker
49:    {
50:        public final String url;
51:        public final int tier;
52:
53:        public Tracker(@NonNull String url, int tier)
54:        {
55:            this.url = url;
56:            this.tier = tier;
57:        }
58:    }
59:
60:    public static final class Progress
61:    {
62:        public final int piece;
63:        public final int numPieces;
64:
65:        public Progress(int piece, int numPieces)
66:        {
67:            this.piece = piece;
68:            this.numPieces = numPieces;
69:        }
70:    }
71:
72:    public enum TorrentVersion {
73:        V1_ONLY (org.libtorrent4j.TorrentBuilder.V1_ONLY),
74:        V2_ONLY (org.libtorrent4j.TorrentBuilder.V2_ONLY),
75:        HYBRID (create_flags_t.from_int(0));
76:
77:        private final create_flags_t flag;
78:
79:        final create_flags_t flag() {
80:            return this.flag;
81:        }
82:
83:        TorrentVersion(create_flags_t flag) {
84:            this.flag = flag;
85:        }
86:    }
87:
88:    public TorrentBuilder(@NonNull Context context)
89:    {
90:        this.context = context;
91:        builder = new org.libtorrent4j.TorrentBuilder();
92:    }
93:
94:    public TorrentBuilder setSeedPath(Uri path) throws UnknownUriException
95:    {
96:        String seedPathStr = SystemFacadeHelper.getFileSystemFacade(context)
97:                .makeFileSystemPath(path);
98:        builder.path(new File(seedPathStr));
99:
100:        return this;
101:    }
102:
103:    /*
104:     * The size of each piece in bytes. It must
105:     * be a multiple of 16 kiB. If a piece size of 0 is specified, a
106:     * piece size will be calculated such that the torrent file is roughly 40 kB
107:     */
108:
109:    public TorrentBuilder setPieceSize(int size)
110:    {
111:        builder.pieceSize(size);
112:
113:        return this;
114:    }
115:
116:    public TorrentBuilder addTrackers(@NonNull List<Tracker> trackers)
117:    {
118:        ArrayList<Pair<String, Integer>> list = new ArrayList<>();
119:        for (Tracker tracker : trackers)
120:            list.add(new Pair<>(tracker.url, tracker.tier));
121:
122:        builder.addTrackers(list);
123:
124:        return this;
125:    }
126:
127:    public TorrentBuilder addUrlSeeds(@NonNull List<String> urls)
128:    {
129:        builder.addUrlSeeds(urls);
130:
131:        return this;
132:    }
133:
134:    public TorrentBuilder setAsPrivate(boolean isPrivate)
135:    {
136:        builder.setPrivate(isPrivate);
137:
138:        return this;
139:    }
140:
141:    public TorrentBuilder setCreator(String creator)
142:    {
143:        builder.creator(creator);
144:
145:        return this;
146:    }
147:
148:    public TorrentBuilder setComment(String comment)
149:    {
150:        builder.comment(comment);
151:
152:        return this;
153:    }
154:
155:    public TorrentBuilder setFileNameFilter(Predicate<String> fileNameFilter)
156:    {
157:        this.fileNameFilter = fileNameFilter;
158:
159:        return this;
160:    }
161:
162:    public TorrentBuilder setTorrentVersion(TorrentVersion version) {
163:        builder.flags(builder.flags().or_(version.flag()));
164:
165:        return this;
166:    }
167:
168:    public Observable<Progress> observeProgress()
169:    {
170:        return progress;
171:    }
172:
173:    public Single<byte[]> build()
174:    {
175:        subscribeProgress();
176:
177:        return Single.fromCallable(() -> builder.generate().entry().bencode());
178:    }
179:
180:    private void subscribeProgress()
181:    {
182:        builder.listener(new org.libtorrent4j.TorrentBuilder.Listener() {
183:            @Override
184:            public boolean accept(String filename)
185:            {
186:                try {
187:                    return fileNameFilter == null || fileNameFilter.test(filename);
188:
189:                } catch (Exception e) {
190:                    return false;
191:                }
192:            }

$ grep -n '' FeedParser.java | sed -n '40,270p'
40:import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
41:import java.util.ArrayList;
42:import java.util.Date;
43:import java.util.List;
44:
45:/*
46: * Parser RSS/Atom feeds.
47: *
48: * Tags are supported for items:
49: *  - <link>
50: *  - <enclosure>
51: *  - <media:content>
52: *  - <media:hash>
53: *  - <guid> (sometimes the torrent link is encoded as the GUID in RSS feeds)
54: *  - ezRSS <torrent: ... > namespace
55: */
56:
57:public class FeedParser
58:{
59:    private FeedChannel feedChannel;
60:    private Feed feed;
61:
62:    public FeedParser(@NonNull Context context, @NonNull FeedChannel feedChannel) throws Exception
63:    {
64:        this.feedChannel = feedChannel;
65:        ByteArrayInputStream bsStream = null;
66:        try {
67:            byte[] response = Utils.fetchHttpUrl(context, feedChannel.url);
68:            if (response == null)
69:                return;
70:            bsStream = new ByteArrayInputStream(response);
71:            feed = FeedParserFactory.newParser().parse(bsStream);
72:
73:        } finally {
74:            if (bsStream != null) {
75:                try {
76:                    bsStream.close();
77:                } catch (Exception e) {
78:                    /* Ignore */
79:                }
80:            }
81:        }
82:    }
83:
84:    public String getTitle()
85:    {
86:        return feed.getTitle();
87:    }
88:
89:    public List<FeedItem> getItems()
90:    {
91:        List<FeedItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
92:        if (feed == null)
93:            return items;
94:
95:        for (Item item : feed.getItemList()) {
96:            List<String> links = item.getLinks();
97:            String articleUrl = getFirstNotNullLink(links);
98:            String downloadUrl = watchDownloadableLink(links);
99:
100:            /* Find url with torrent/magnet */
101:            if (downloadUrl == null)
102:                downloadUrl = findDownloadUrl(item);
103:
104:            Date pubDate = item.getPubDate();
105:            long pubDateTime = 0;
106:            if (pubDate != null)
107:                pubDateTime = pubDate.getTime();
108:
109:            FeedItem feedItem = new FeedItem(feedChannel.id, downloadUrl,
110:                    articleUrl, item.getTitle(), pubDateTime);
111:            feedItem.fetchDate = System.currentTimeMillis();
112:            items.add(feedItem);
113:        }
114:
115:        return items;
116:    }
117:
118:    private String getFirstNotNullLink(List<String> links)
119:    {
120:        for (String link : links) {
121:            if (!TextUtils.isEmpty(link))
122:                return link;
123:        }
124:
125:        return null;
126:    }
127:
128:    private String watchDownloadableLink(List<String> links)
129:    {
130:        for (String link : links) {
131:            if (link == null)
132:                continue;
133:
134:            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(link))
135:                return link;
136:        }
137:
138:        return null;
139:    }
140:
141:    private String findDownloadUrl(Item item)
142:    {
143:        /*
144:         * Watch tags in descending order of importance
145:         * (hashes in the last place)
146:         */
147:        String enclosureUrl = watchEnclosure(item);
148:        if (enclosureUrl != null)
149:            return enclosureUrl;
150:
151:        String infoHash = watchTorrentInfoHash(item);
152:        if (infoHash != null)
153:            return infoHash;
154:
155:        String mediaContentUrl = watchMediaContent(item);
156:        if (mediaContentUrl != null)
157:            return mediaContentUrl;
158:
159:        String guid = watchGuid(item);
160:        return guid;
161:    }
162:
163:    /*
164:     * Parse element like this:
165:     * <enclosure type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
166:     */
167:
168:    private String watchEnclosure(Item item)
169:    {
170:        for (Enclosure enclosure : item.getEnclosures()) {
171:            if (enclosure == null)
172:                continue;
173:
174:            String url = enclosure.getUrl();
175:            String type = enclosure.getType();
176:            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
177:                return url;
178:        }
179:
180:        return null;
181:    }
182:
183:    /*
184:     * Parse element like this:
185:     * <media:content type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
186:     */
187:
188:    private String watchMediaContent(Item item)
189:    {
190:        MediaRss media = item.getMediaRss();
191:        if (media == null)
192:            return null;
193:
194:        List<Content> contentList = media.getContent();
195:        for (Content content : contentList) {
196:            if (content == null)
197:                continue;
198:
199:            String url = content.getUrl();
200:            if (url == null)
201:                return watchMediaHash(media);
202:            String type = content.getType();
203:            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
204:                return url;
205:        }
206:
207:        return watchMediaHash(media);
208:    }
209:
210:    /*
211:     * Parse element like this:
212:     * <media:hash algo="sha1">8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</media:hash>
213:     */
214:
215:    private String watchMediaHash(MediaRss media)
216:    {
217:        Hash hash = media.getHash();
218:        if (hash == null)
219:            return null;
220:
221:        String hashStr = hash.getValue();
222:        if (hashStr == null)
223:            return null;
224:        String algo = hash.getAlgorithm();
225:        if (Utils.isHash(hashStr) && algo != null && algo.equalsIgnoreCase("sha1"))
226:            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(hashStr);
227:
228:        return null;
229:    }
230:
231:    /*
232:     * Parse element like this:
233:     * <torrent:infoHash>8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</torrent:infoHash>
234:     */
235:
236:    private String watchTorrentInfoHash(Item item)
237:    {
238:        EzRssTorrentItem torrentItem = item.getEzRssTorrentItem();
239:        if (torrentItem == null)
240:            return null;
241:
242:        String infoHash = torrentItem.getInfoHash();
243:        if (infoHash != null && Utils.isHash(infoHash))
244:            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(infoHash);
245:
246:        return null;
247:    }
248:
249:    /*
250:     * Parse element like this:
251:     * <guid>http://foo.com/bar.torrent</guid>
252:     */
253:
254:    private String watchGuid(Item item)
255:    {
256:        String url = item.getGuid();
257:        if (url != null && isMagnetOrTorrent(url))
258:            return url;
259:
260:        return null;
261:    }
262:
263:    private boolean isMagnetOrTorrent(String url)
264:    {
265:        return url.endsWith(".torrent") || url.startsWith(Utils.MAGNET_PREFIX);
266:    }
267:}

21:package org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core;
22:
23:import org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core.utils.Utils;
24:
25:import java.io.IOException;
26:import java.net.HttpURLConnection;
27:import java.net.MalformedURLException;
28:import java.net.URL;
29:import java.security.GeneralSecurityException;
30:
31:import javax.net.ssl.HttpsURLConnection;
32:import javax.net.ssl.SSLContext;
33:
34:import static android.text.format.DateUtils.SECOND_IN_MILLIS;
35:import static java.net.HttpURLConnection.HTTP_MOVED_PERM;
36:import static java.net.HttpURLConnection.HTTP_MOVED_TEMP;
37:import static java.net.HttpURLConnection.HTTP_SEE_OTHER;
38:
39:/*
40: * The wrapper for HttpUrlConnection.
41: */
42:
43:public class HttpConnection implements Runnable
44:{
45:    /* Can't be more than 7 */
46:    private static final int MAX_REDIRECTS = 5;
47:    private static final int DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = (int)(20 * SECOND_IN_MILLIS);
48:
49:    private URL url;
50:    private SSLContext sslContext;
51:    private Listener listener;
52:
53:    public interface Listener
54:    {
55:        void onConnectionCreated(HttpURLConnection conn);
56:
57:        void onResponseHandle(HttpURLConnection conn, int code, String message);
58:
59:        void onMovedPermanently(String newUrl);
60:
61:        void onIOException(IOException e);
62:
63:        void onTooManyRedirects();
64:    }
65:
66:    public HttpConnection(String url) throws MalformedURLException, GeneralSecurityException
67:    {
68:        this.url = new URL(url);
69:        this.sslContext = Utils.getSSLContext();
70:    }
71:
72:    public void setListener(Listener listener)
73:    {
74:        this.listener = listener;
75:    }
76:
77:    @Override
78:    public void run()
79:    {
80:        int redirectionCount = 0;
81:        while (redirectionCount++ < MAX_REDIRECTS) {
82:            HttpURLConnection conn = null;
83:            try {
84:                conn = (HttpURLConnection)url.openConnection();
85:                conn.setInstanceFollowRedirects(false);
86:                conn.setConnectTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT);
87:                conn.setReadTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT);
88:
89:                if (conn instanceof HttpsURLConnection)
90:                    ((HttpsURLConnection)conn).setSSLSocketFactory(sslContext.getSocketFactory());
91:
92:                if (listener != null)
93:                    listener.onConnectionCreated(conn);
94:
95:                int responseCode = conn.getResponseCode();
96:                switch (responseCode) {
97:                    case HTTP_MOVED_PERM:
98:                    case HTTP_MOVED_TEMP:
99:                    case HTTP_SEE_OTHER:
100:                        String location = conn.getHeaderField("Location");
101:                        url = new URL(url, location);
102:                        if (responseCode == HTTP_MOVED_PERM && listener != null)
103:                            listener.onMovedPermanently(url.toString());
104:                        continue;
105:                    default:
106:                        if (listener != null)
107:                            listener.onResponseHandle(conn, responseCode, conn.getResponseMessage());
108:                        return;
109:                }
110:
111:            } catch (IOException e) {
112:                if (listener != null)
113:                    listener.onIOException(e);
114:                return;
115:
116:            } finally {
117:                if (conn != null)
118:                    conn.disconnect();
119:            }
120:        }
121:
122:        if (listener != null)
123:            listener.onTooManyRedirects();
124:    }
125:}

462:    public static byte[] fetchHttpUrl(@NonNull Context context,
463:                                      @NonNull String url) throws FetchLinkException {
464:        byte[][] response = new byte[1][];
465:
466:        if (!Utils.checkConnectivity(context))
467:            throw new FetchLinkException("No network connection");
468:
469:        final ArrayList<Throwable> errorArray = new ArrayList<>(1);
470:        HttpConnection connection;
471:        try {
472:            connection = new HttpConnection(url);
473:        } catch (Exception e) {
474:            throw new FetchLinkException(e);
475:        }
476:
477:        connection.setListener(new HttpConnection.Listener() {
478:            @Override
479:            public void onConnectionCreated(HttpURLConnection conn) {
480:                /* Nothing */
481:            }
482:
483:            @Override
484:            public void onResponseHandle(HttpURLConnection conn, int code, String message) {
485:                if (code == HttpURLConnection.HTTP_OK) {
486:                    try(InputStream is = conn.getInputStream()) {
487:                        response[0] = IOUtils.toByteArray(is);
488:
489:                    } catch (IOException e) {
490:                        errorArray.add(e);
491:                    }
492:                } else {
493:                    errorArray.add(new FetchLinkException("Failed to fetch link, response code: " + code));
494:                }
495:            }
496:
497:            @Override
498:            public void onMovedPermanently(String newUrl) {
499:                /* Nothing */
500:            }
501:
502:            @Override
503:            public void onIOException(IOException e) {
504:                errorArray.add(e);
505:            }
506:
507:            @Override
508:            public void onTooManyRedirects() {
509:                errorArray.add(new FetchLinkException("Too many redirects"));
510:            }
511:        });
512:        connection.run();
513:
514:        if (!errorArray.isEmpty()) {
515:            StringBuilder s = new StringBuilder();
516:            for (Throwable e : errorArray) {
517:                String msg = e.getMessage();
518:                if (msg != null)
519:                    s.append(msg.concat("\n"));
520:            }
521:
522:            throw new FetchLinkException(s.toString());
523:        }
524:
525:        return response[0];

28: * Filtering numbers, which are outside the specified range.
29: */
30:
31:public class InputFilterRange implements InputFilter
32:{
33:    public static final InputFilterRange PORT_FILTER = new InputFilterRange.Builder()
34:            .setMin(0)
35:            .setMax(65535)
36:            .build();
37:
38:    public static final InputFilterRange UNSIGNED_INT = new InputFilterRange.Builder()
39:            .setMin(0)
40:            .setMax(Integer.MAX_VALUE)
41:            .build();
42:
43:    private Integer min;
44:    private Integer max;
45:
46:    private InputFilterRange(Integer min, Integer max)
47:    {
48:        this.min = min;
49:        this.max = max;
50:    }
51:
52:    @Nullable
53:    public Integer getMin()
54:    {
55:        return min;
56:    }
57:
58:    @Nullable
59:    public Integer getMax()
60:    {
61:        return max;
62:    }
63:
64:    @Override
65:    public CharSequence filter(CharSequence source, int start, int end,
66:                               Spanned dest, int dstart, int dend)
67:    {
68:        if (source.length() == 1 && source.charAt(0) == '-')
69:            return null;
70:
71:        try {
72:            int input = Integer.parseInt(dest.toString() + source.toString());
73:            if (inRange(input))
74:                return null;
75:
76:        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
77:            /* Ignore */
78:        }
79:
80:        return "";
81:    }
82:
83:    private boolean inRange(int num)
84:    {
85:        return (min == null || num >= min) && (max == null || num <= max);
86:    }
87:
88:    public static class Builder
89:    {
90:        private Integer min;
91:        private Integer max;
92:
93:        public Builder setMin(int min)
94:        {
95:            this.min = min;
96:
97:            return this;
98:        }
99:
100:        public Builder setMax(int max)
101:        {
102:            this.max = max;
103:
104:            return this;
105:        }
106:
107:        public InputFilterRange build()
108:        {
109:            if (min != null && max != null && min > max)
110:                throw new IllegalArgumentException("max < min");
111:
112:            return new InputFilterRange(min, max);
113:        }
114:    }

51:                .withBuildConfigClass(BuildConfig.class)
52:                .withReportFormat(StringFormat.JSON);

54:                .withMailTo("proninyaroslav@mail.ru")
55:                .build());
56:        builder.withPluginConfigurations(new DialogConfigurationBuilder()
57:                .withEnabled(true)
58:                .withReportDialogClass(ErrorReportActivity.class)
59:                .build());
60:        // Set stub handler
61:        if (Thread.getDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler() == null) {
62:            Thread.setDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler((t, e) ->
63:                    Log.e(TAG, "Uncaught exception in " + t + ": " + Log.getStackTraceString(e))
64:            );
65:        }
66:        ACRA.init(this, builder);
67:
68:        increaseCursorWindowSize();
69:
70:        TorrentNotifier.getInstance(this).makeNotifyChans();
71:    }
72:
73:    @SuppressLint("DiscouragedPrivateApi")
74:    private void increaseCursorWindowSize() {
75:        try {
76:            var field = CursorWindow.class.getDeclaredField("sCursorWindowSize");
77:            field.setAccessible(true);
78:            field.set(null, 100 * 1024 * 1024); // 100MB
79:        } catch (Exception e) {
80:            Log.e(TAG, "Unable to increase CursorWindow size", e);
81:        }
82:    }

$ grep -n '' core/utils/Utils.java | sed -n '155,163p;460,526p'
155:    }
156:
157:    public static boolean checkConnectivity(@NonNull Context context) {
158:        SystemFacade systemFacade = SystemFacadeHelper.getSystemFacade(context);
159:        NetworkInfo netInfo = systemFacade.getActiveNetworkInfo();
160:
161:        return netInfo != null && netInfo.isConnected() && isNetworkTypeAllowed(context);
162:    }
163:
460:    }
461:
462:    public static byte[] fetchHttpUrl(@NonNull Context context,
463:                                      @NonNull String url) throws FetchLinkException {
464:        byte[][] response = new byte[1][];
465:
466:        if (!Utils.checkConnectivity(context))
467:            throw new FetchLinkException("No network connection");
468:
469:        final ArrayList<Throwable> errorArray = new ArrayList<>(1);
470:        HttpConnection connection;
471:        try {
472:            connection = new HttpConnection(url);
473:        } catch (Exception e) {
474:            throw new FetchLinkException(e);
475:        }
476:
477:        connection.setListener(new HttpConnection.Listener() {
478:            @Override
479:            public void onConnectionCreated(HttpURLConnection conn) {
480:                /* Nothing */
481:            }
482:
483:            @Override
484:            public void onResponseHandle(HttpURLConnection conn, int code, String message) {
485:                if (code == HttpURLConnection.HTTP_OK) {
486:                    try(InputStream is = conn.getInputStream()) {
487:                        response[0] = IOUtils.toByteArray(is);
488:
489:                    } catch (IOException e) {
490:                        errorArray.add(e);
491:                    }
492:                } else {
493:                    errorArray.add(new FetchLinkException("Failed to fetch link, response code: " + code));
494:                }
495:            }
496:
497:            @Override
498:            public void onMovedPermanently(String newUrl) {
499:                /* Nothing */
500:            }
501:
502:            @Override
503:            public void onIOException(IOException e) {
504:                errorArray.add(e);
505:            }
506:
507:            @Override
508:            public void onTooManyRedirects() {
509:                errorArray.add(new FetchLinkException("Too many redirects"));
510:            }
511:        });
512:        connection.run();
513:
514:        if (!errorArray.isEmpty()) {
515:            StringBuilder s = new StringBuilder();
516:            for (Throwable e : errorArray) {
517:                String msg = e.getMessage();
518:                if (msg != null)
519:                    s.append(msg.concat("\n"));
520:            }
521:
522:            throw new FetchLinkException(s.toString());
523:        }
524:
525:        return response[0];
526:    }

$ grep -n '' core/HttpConnection.java | sed -n '20,126p'

21:package org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core;
22:
23:import org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core.utils.Utils;
24:
25:import java.io.IOException;
26:import java.net.HttpURLConnection;
27:import java.net.MalformedURLException;
28:import java.net.URL;
29:import java.security.GeneralSecurityException;
30:
31:import javax.net.ssl.HttpsURLConnection;
32:import javax.net.ssl.SSLContext;
33:
34:import static android.text.format.DateUtils.SECOND_IN_MILLIS;
35:import static java.net.HttpURLConnection.HTTP_MOVED_PERM;
36:import static java.net.HttpURLConnection.HTTP_MOVED_TEMP;
37:import static java.net.HttpURLConnection.HTTP_SEE_OTHER;
38:
39:/*
40: * The wrapper for HttpUrlConnection.
41: */
42:
43:public class HttpConnection implements Runnable
44:{
45:    /* Can't be more than 7 */
46:    private static final int MAX_REDIRECTS = 5;
47:    private static final int DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = (int)(20 * SECOND_IN_MILLIS);
48:
49:    private URL url;
50:    private SSLContext sslContext;
51:    private Listener listener;
52:
53:    public interface Listener
54:    {
55:        void onConnectionCreated(HttpURLConnection conn);
56:
57:        void onResponseHandle(HttpURLConnection conn, int code, String message);
58:
59:        void onMovedPermanently(String newUrl);
60:
61:        void onIOException(IOException e);
62:
63:        void onTooManyRedirects();
64:    }
65:
66:    public HttpConnection(String url) throws MalformedURLException, GeneralSecurityException
67:    {
68:        this.url = new URL(url);
69:        this.sslContext = Utils.getSSLContext();
70:    }
71:
72:    public void setListener(Listener listener)
73:    {
74:        this.listener = listener;
75:    }
76:
77:    @Override
78:    public void run()
79:    {
80:        int redirectionCount = 0;
81:        while (redirectionCount++ < MAX_REDIRECTS) {
82:            HttpURLConnection conn = null;
83:            try {
84:                conn = (HttpURLConnection)url.openConnection();
85:                conn.setInstanceFollowRedirects(false);
86:                conn.setConnectTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT);
87:                conn.setReadTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT);
88:
89:                if (conn instanceof HttpsURLConnection)
90:                    ((HttpsURLConnection)conn).setSSLSocketFactory(sslContext.getSocketFactory());
91:
92:                if (listener != null)
93:                    listener.onConnectionCreated(conn);
94:
95:                int responseCode = conn.getResponseCode();
96:                switch (responseCode) {
97:                    case HTTP_MOVED_PERM:
98:                    case HTTP_MOVED_TEMP:
99:                    case HTTP_SEE_OTHER:
100:                        String location = conn.getHeaderField("Location");
101:                        url = new URL(url, location);
102:                        if (responseCode == HTTP_MOVED_PERM && listener != null)
103:                            listener.onMovedPermanently(url.toString());
104:                        continue;
105:                    default:
106:                        if (listener != null)
107:                            listener.onResponseHandle(conn, responseCode, conn.getResponseMessage());
108:                        return;
109:                }
110:
111:            } catch (IOException e) {
112:                if (listener != null)
113:                    listener.onIOException(e);
114:                return;
115:
116:            } finally {
117:                if (conn != null)
118:                    conn.disconnect();
119:            }
120:        }
121:
122:        if (listener != null)
123:            listener.onTooManyRedirects();
124:    }
125:}

22:import android.text.InputFilter;
23:import android.text.Spanned;
24:
25:import androidx.annotation.Nullable;
26:
27:/*
28: * Filtering numbers, which are outside the specified range.
29: */
30:
31:public class InputFilterRange implements InputFilter
32:{
33:    public static final InputFilterRange PORT_FILTER = new InputFilterRange.Builder()
34:            .setMin(0)
35:            .setMax(65535)
36:            .build();
37:
38:    public static final InputFilterRange UNSIGNED_INT = new InputFilterRange.Builder()
39:            .setMin(0)
40:            .setMax(Integer.MAX_VALUE)
41:            .build();
42:
43:    private Integer min;
44:    private Integer max;
45:
46:    private InputFilterRange(Integer min, Integer max)
47:    {
48:        this.min = min;
49:        this.max = max;
50:    }
51:
52:    @Nullable
53:    public Integer getMin()
54:    {
55:        return min;
56:    }
57:
58:    @Nullable
59:    public Integer getMax()
60:    {
61:        return max;
62:    }
63:
64:    @Override
65:    public CharSequence filter(CharSequence source, int start, int end,
66:                               Spanned dest, int dstart, int dend)
67:    {
68:        if (source.length() == 1 && source.charAt(0) == '-')
69:            return null;
70:
71:        try {
72:            int input = Integer.parseInt(dest.toString() + source.toString());
73:            if (inRange(input))
74:                return null;
75:
76:        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
77:            /* Ignore */
78:        }
79:
80:        return "";
81:    }
82:
83:    private boolean inRange(int num)
84:    {
85:        return (min == null || num >= min) && (max == null || num <= max);
86:    }
87:
88:    public static class Builder
89:    {
90:        private Integer min;
91:        private Integer max;
92:
93:        public Builder setMin(int min)
94:        {
95:            this.min = min;
96:
97:            return this;
98:        }
99:
100:        public Builder setMax(int max)
101:        {
102:            this.max = max;
103:
104:            return this;
105:        }
106:
107:        public InputFilterRange build()
108:        {
109:            if (min != null && max != null && min > max)
110:                throw new IllegalArgumentException("max < min");
111:
112:            return new InputFilterRange(min, max);
113:        }
114:    }
115:}

62:    public FeedParser(@NonNull Context context, @NonNull FeedChannel feedChannel) throws Exception
63:    {

69:                return;

75:                try {

89:    public List<FeedItem> getItems()
90:    {
91:        List<FeedItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
92:        if (feed == null)
93:            return items;
94:
95:        for (Item item : feed.getItemList()) {
96:            List<String> links = item.getLinks();
97:            String articleUrl = getFirstNotNullLink(links);
98:            String downloadUrl = watchDownloadableLink(links);
99:
100:            /* Find url with torrent/magnet */
101:            if (downloadUrl == null)
102:                downloadUrl = findDownloadUrl(item);
103:
104:            Date pubDate = item.getPubDate();
105:            long pubDateTime = 0;
106:            if (pubDate != null)
107:                pubDateTime = pubDate.getTime();
108:
109:            FeedItem feedItem = new FeedItem(feedChannel.id, downloadUrl,
110:                    articleUrl, item.getTitle(), pubDateTime);
111:            feedItem.fetchDate = System.currentTimeMillis();
112:            items.add(feedItem);
113:        }
114:
115:        return items;
116:    }

118:    private String getFirstNotNullLink(List<String> links)
119:    {
120:        for (String link : links) {
140:
141:    private String findDownloadUrl(Item item)
142:    {
143:        /*
144:         * Watch tags in descending order of importance
145:         * (hashes in the last place)
146:         */
147:        String enclosureUrl = watchEnclosure(item);
148:        if (enclosureUrl != null)
149:            return enclosureUrl;
150:
151:        String infoHash = watchTorrentInfoHash(item);
152:        if (infoHash != null)
153:            return infoHash;
154:
155:        String mediaContentUrl = watchMediaContent(item);
156:        if (mediaContentUrl != null)
157:            return mediaContentUrl;
158:
159:        String guid = watchGuid(item);
160:        return guid;
161:    }
162:
163:    /*
164:     * Parse element like this:
165:     * <enclosure type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
166:     */
167:
168:    private String watchEnclosure(Item item)
169:    {
170:        for (Enclosure enclosure : item.getEnclosures()) {
171:            if (enclosure == null)
172:                continue;
173:
174:            String url = enclosure.getUrl();
175:            String type = enclosure.getType();
176:            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
177:                return url;
178:        }
179:
180:        return null;
230:
231:    /*
232:     * Parse element like this:
233:     * <torrent:infoHash>8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</torrent:infoHash>
234:     */
235:
236:    private String watchTorrentInfoHash(Item item)
237:    {
238:        EzRssTorrentItem torrentItem = item.getEzRssTorrentItem();
239:        if (torrentItem == null)
240:            return null;
241:
242:        String infoHash = torrentItem.getInfoHash();
243:        if (infoHash != null && Utils.isHash(infoHash))
244:            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(infoHash);
245:
246:        return null;
247:    }
248:
249:    /*
250:     * Parse element like this:
251:     * <guid>http://foo.com/bar.torrent</guid>
252:     */
253:
254:    private String watchGuid(Item item)
255:    {
256:        String url = item.getGuid();
257:        if (url != null && isMagnetOrTorrent(url))
258:            return url;
259:
260:        return null;
261:    }
262:
263:    private boolean isMagnetOrTorrent(String url)
264:    {
265:        return url.endsWith(".torrent") || url.startsWith(Utils.MAGNET_PREFIX);
266:    }
267:}

188:    private String watchMediaContent(Item item)
189:    {
190:        MediaRss media = item.getMediaRss();
191:        if (media == null)
192:            return null;
193:
194:        List<Content> contentList = media.getContent();
195:        for (Content content : contentList) {
196:            if (content == null)
197:                continue;
198:
199:            String url = content.getUrl();
200:            if (url == null)
201:                return watchMediaHash(media);
202:            String type = content.getType();
203:            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
204:                return url;
205:        }
206:
207:        return watchMediaHash(media);
208:    }
209:
210:    /*
211:     * Parse element like this:
212:     * <media:hash algo="sha1">8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</media:hash>
213:     */
214:
215:    private String watchMediaHash(MediaRss media)
216:    {
217:        Hash hash = media.getHash();
218:        if (hash == null)
219:            return null;
220:
221:        String hashStr = hash.getValue();
222:        if (hashStr == null)
223:            return null;
224:        String algo = hash.getAlgorithm();
225:        if (Utils.isHash(hashStr) && algo != null && algo.equalsIgnoreCase("sha1"))
226:            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(hashStr);
227:
228:        return null;
229:    }

124:    private Result fetchChannels(List<FeedChannel> channels, long acceptMinDate,
125:                                 boolean noAutoDownload)
126:    {
127:        if (channels == null)

133:                continue;
134:            results.add(fetchChannel(channel.id, acceptMinDate, noAutoDownload));
135:        }

139:                return result;
140:

    private Result fetchChannel(long id, long acceptMinDate,
                                boolean noAutoDownload)
    {
        if (id == -1)
            return Result.failure();

        FeedChannel channel = repo.getFeedById(id);
        if (channel == null)
            return Result.failure();

        FeedParser parser;
        try {
            parser = new FeedParser(getApplicationContext(), channel);

        } catch (Exception e) {
            channel.fetchError = e.getMessage();
            repo.updateFeed(channel);

    20	    @Override
    21	    public CharSequence filter(CharSequence source, int start, int end,
    22	                               Spanned dest, int dstart, int dend)
    23	    {
    24	        if (source.length() == 1 && source.charAt(0) == '-')
    25	            return null;
    26	
    27	        try {
    28	            int input = Integer.parseInt(dest.toString() + source.toString());
    29	            if (inRange(input))
    30	                return null;
    31	
    32	        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
    33	            /* Ignore */
    34	        }
    35	
    36	        return "";
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    private boolean inRange(int num)
    40	    {
    41	        return (min == null || num >= min) && (max == null || num <= max);
    42	    }
    43	        public InputFilterRange build()
    44	        {
    45	            if (min != null && max != null && min > max)
    46	                throw new IllegalArgumentException("max < min");
    47	
    48	            return new InputFilterRange(min, max);
    49	        }

$ sed FeedParser.java 89-96 and 141-262 (with file line numbers)
89	    public List<FeedItem> getItems()
90	    {
91	        List<FeedItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
92	        if (feed == null)
93	            return items;
94	
95	        for (Item item : feed.getItemList()) {
96	            List<String> links = item.getLinks();
141	    private String findDownloadUrl(Item item)
142	    {
143	        /*
144	         * Watch tags in descending order of importance
145	         * (hashes in the last place)
146	         */
147	        String enclosureUrl = watchEnclosure(item);
148	        if (enclosureUrl != null)
149	            return enclosureUrl;
150	
151	        String infoHash = watchTorrentInfoHash(item);
152	        if (infoHash != null)
153	            return infoHash;
154	
155	        String mediaContentUrl = watchMediaContent(item);
156	        if (mediaContentUrl != null)
157	            return mediaContentUrl;
158	
159	        String guid = watchGuid(item);
160	        return guid;
161	    }
162	
163	    /*
164	     * Parse element like this:
165	     * <enclosure type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
166	     */
167	
168	    private String watchEnclosure(Item item)
169	    {
170	        for (Enclosure enclosure : item.getEnclosures()) {
171	            if (enclosure == null)
172	                continue;
173	
174	            String url = enclosure.getUrl();
175	            String type = enclosure.getType();
176	            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
177	                return url;
178	        }
179	
180	        return null;
181	    }
182	
183	    /*
184	     * Parse element like this:
185	     * <media:content type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
186	     */
187	
188	    private String watchMediaContent(Item item)
189	    {
190	        MediaRss media = item.getMediaRss();
191	        if (media == null)
192	            return null;
193	
194	        List<Content> contentList = media.getContent();
195	        for (Content content : contentList) {
196	            if (content == null)
197	                continue;
198	
199	            String url = content.getUrl();
200	            if (url == null)
201	                return watchMediaHash(media);
202	            String type = content.getType();
203	            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
204	                return url;
205	        }
206	
207	        return watchMediaHash(media);
208	    }
209	
210	    /*
211	     * Parse element like this:
212	     * <media:hash algo="sha1">8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</media:hash>
213	     */
214	
215	    private String watchMediaHash(MediaRss media)
216	    {
217	        Hash hash = media.getHash();
218	        if (hash == null)
219	            return null;
220	
221	        String hashStr = hash.getValue();
222	        if (hashStr == null)
223	            return null;
224	        String algo = hash.getAlgorithm();
225	        if (Utils.isHash(hashStr) && algo != null && algo.equalsIgnoreCase("sha1"))
226	            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(hashStr);
227	
228	        return null;
229	    }
230	
231	    /*
232	     * Parse element like this:
233	     * <torrent:infoHash>8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</torrent:infoHash>
234	     */
235	
236	    private String watchTorrentInfoHash(Item item)
237	    {
238	        EzRssTorrentItem torrentItem = item.getEzRssTorrentItem();
239	        if (torrentItem == null)
240	            return null;
241	
242	        String infoHash = torrentItem.getInfoHash();
243	        if (infoHash != null && Utils.isHash(infoHash))
244	            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(infoHash);
245	
246	        return null;
247	    }
248	
249	    /*
250	     * Parse element like this:
251	     * <guid>http://foo.com/bar.torrent</guid>
252	     */
253	
254	    private String watchGuid(Item item)
255	    {
256	        String url = item.getGuid();
257	        if (url != null && isMagnetOrTorrent(url))
258	            return url;
259	
260	        return null;
261	    }
262

74	    private void increaseCursorWindowSize() {
